local _ce4e636d = {}

local function _decode(s)
    s = s:gsub("[^%w%+%/%=]", "")
    local out = {}
    local n = 0
    local buf = 0
    for i = 1, #s do
        local ch = s:sub(i, i)
        if ch ~= "=" then
            local v = string.find(_8c326f53, ch, 1, true)
            if v then
                v = v - 1
                buf = buf * 64 + v
                n = n + 6
                if n >= 8 then
                    n = n - 8
                    out[#out + 1] = string.char(math.floor(buf / 2^n) % 256)
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return table.concat(out)
end
